- **harness-agents-v2-diagnoser**: Use when the harness_agents_v2 director delegates diagnosis after a real failure. - **harness-agents-v2-director**: Use when orchestrating a software development task following the harness_agents_v2 flow. The main agent (you) becomes DIRECTOR: classify the request, load the relevant specialist skills in order, run the diff-first TDD loop, and close the gate. Triggers on 'implement X following the harness', 'start a new module with director', or any development task that needs the 14-agent flow. ([pinotronic/software-development](https://github.com/pinotronic/software-development/tree/HEAD/skills/software-development/harness-agents-v2-director))
- **harness-agents-v2-domain-analyst**: Use when the harness_agents_v2 director delegates the domain-modeling stage. ([pinotronic/software-development](https://github.com/pinotronic/software-development/tree/HEAD/skills/software-development/harness-agents-v2-domain-analyst))
- **harness-agents-v2-implementer**: Use when the harness_agents_v2 director delegates the production patch stage. Output is a diff applied by the director. ([pinotronic/software-development](https://github.com/pinotronic/software-development/tree/HEAD/skills/software-development/harness-agents-v2-implementer))
- **harness-agents-v2-logger**: Use when the harness_agents_v2 director needs to record any event of the flow: agent invocation, test run, patch applied, blocker, or session boundary. Writes a strict JSON line to ~/harness-agents-v2-logs/&lt;proyecto&gt;/events.jsonl or tests.jsonl. The director should call this on every meaningful step, even when the model is in degraded mode, so the user can audit progress with harness-agents-v2-status. ([pinotronic/software-development](https://github.com/pinotronic/software-development/tree/HEAD/skills/software-development/harness-agents-v2-logger))
- **harness-agents-v2-planner**: Use when the harness_agents_v2 director delegates the per-ticket plan. Do not design whole modules, do not write code, no patches. Read-only. ([pinotronic/software-development](https://github.com/pinotronic/software-development/tree/HEAD/skills/software-development/harness-agents-v2-planner))
- **harness-agents-v2-requirements-analyst**: Use when the harness_agents_v2 director delegates the requirements stage. Read-only, no architecture or stories. ([pinotronic/software-development](https://github.com/pinotronic/software-development/tree/HEAD/skills/software-development/harness-agents-v2-requirements-analyst))
- **harness-agents-v2-reviewer**: Use when the harness_agents_v2 director delegates the closing gate. Inspect plan, tests, production patch, applied diff, and logs. Emit APROBADO / RECHAZADO / APROBADO CON OBSERVACIONES plus a valid REVIEW_FINDINGS JSON block. Read-only, decides if the ticket can be closed. ([pinotronic/software-development](https://github.com/pinotronic/software-development/tree/HEAD/skills/software-development/harness-agents-v2-reviewer))
- **harness-agents-v2-spec-writer**: Use when the harness_agents_v2 director delegates the spec stage. Includes inputs, outputs, errors, validations, compatibility, verifiable criteria. Read-only. ([pinotronic/software-development](https://github.com/pinotronic/software-development/tree/HEAD/skills/software-development/harness-agents-v2-spec-writer))
- **harness-agents-v2-sprint-planner**: Use when the harness_agents_v2 director delegates the sprint stage.
